### [1] [The Neutron Electric Dipole Moment from Lattice QCD using a Background Electric Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10606)
*Thomas Blum,Fangcheng He,Taku Izubuchi,Luchang Jin,Hiroshi Ohki,Sergey Syritsyn*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过格点QCD计算中子电偶极矩（nEDM）$d_n$，采用2+1味域壁费米子组态，在固定格距$a\approx 0.11\,\text{fm}$及多个π介子质量下，结合CP破坏的$\bar\theta Q_{\text{top}}$项与静态外电场，利用Feynman-Hellmann定理改进拓扑荷密度单时间片采样，构造非厄米广义本征问题以压制激发态污染，最终外推得$d_n = -0.0050(4)^{\text{stat}}(8)^{\text{sys}}\, \bar\theta\, e\,\text{fm}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定中子电偶极矩对检验标准模型外CP破坏机制（如强CP问题）至关重要；格点QCD是唯一第一性原理方法，但传统方法受统计噪声和激发态污染严重制约。

Method: 基于2+1味域壁费米子格点组态；引入静态均匀背景电场与$\bar\theta Q_{\text{top}}$项耦合；利用Feynman-Hellmann定理，改用单时间片拓扑荷密度$q_{\text{top}}(t)$而非全局$Q_{\text{top}}$以提升统计精度；计算形变核子中拓扑荷密度前向矩阵元；构造非厄米广义本征问题提取基态，消除正宇称投影算符导致的激发态污染；系统考察不同插值算符（手征协变/非协变）一致性。

Result: $d_n = -0.0050(4)^{\text{stat}}(8)^{\text{sys}}\, \bar\theta\, e\,\text{fm}$，其中统计误差来自蒙特卡洛采样，系统误差主要源于激发态拟合区间变化及外电场强度依赖性；结果在不同核子插值算符下一致。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实现了基于单时间片拓扑荷密度与非厄米本征分析的高精度nEDM格点计算，显著抑制了统计涨落与激发态污染，为后续包含离散化、有限体积与手征外推的完整系统误差分析奠定基础。

Abstract: We present the calculation of the neutron electric dipole moment (nEDM) $d_n$ using 2+1 flavor domain wall fermion ensembles with fixed lattice spacing $a\approx 0.11\,\text{fm}$ and pion masses of 340, 420, and 576 MeV. We show that the neutron electric dipole moment can be extracted from the energy shift induced by a static uniform external background electric field in the presence of the CP-violating QCD theta-term, $\barθQ_{top}$. Motivated by the Feynman-Hellmann theorem, we employ sampling of the topological charge $q_\text{top}(t)$ on a single time-slice rather than the global topological charge $Q_\text{top}=\int q_\text{top}(t) \, dt$, which dramatically improves the statistical precision of the $θ$-induced nEDM. Key to our method is to calculate the forward matrix element of the topological charge density in the nucleon deformed by a background electric field. We find that calculation with the traditional positive parity-projected nucleon operator is subject to large excited-state contamination. To remove the contamination, we construct the ground state of the deformed nucleon by solving a non-Hermitian generalized eigenvalue problem. With this approach, we find consistent values for the nEDM when using different nucleon interpolating operators, regardless of whether they are covariant or non-covariant under chiral transformations. Finally, after extrapolating to the physical point, we obtain $d_n=-0.0050(4)^\text{stat}(8)^\text{sys}\barθ$ $e$ fm, where the systematic uncertainty includes excited-state effects estimated as variation with the Euclidean-time fits and the dependence on the strength of the electric field applied to the neutron. Conventional systematic errors like discretization, finite-volume, and chiral extrapolation effects will be addressed in future work.

</details>


### [2] [Chiral and $U(1)_A$ symmetries in background magnetic fields from lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11625)
*Heng-Tong Ding,José Javier Hernández Hernández,Dan Zhang*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文利用格点QCD研究强磁场下QCD中的手征对称性和单态U(1)_A对称性，定义并计算了中性π⁰–σ和π⁰–δ⁰磁化率差作为手征与U(1)_A对称性破缺的探针，发现其随磁场变化呈现磁催化与逆磁催化行为，并首次给出中性扇区相关磁化率劈裂的格点结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解外加磁场下QCD相结构与对称性恢复机制，特别是手征对称性与U(1)_A反常对称性的响应，需区分中性扇区中因夸克电荷差异导致的对称性破缺模式，并厘清磁化率劈裂与磁催化/逆磁催化现象的关联。

Method: 在固定尺度的(2+1)味HISQ格点系综上（m_l = m_s^phys/10，m_π ≈ 220 MeV），构造中性π⁰、σ、δ⁰介子通道的连通与非连通磁化率，定义χ_{π⁰}−χ_σ为中性非单态手征伙伴劈裂，χ_{π⁰}−χ_{δ⁰}为U(1)_A单态伙伴劈裂；系统分析其随磁场强度eB与温度的变化行为。

Result: 中性手征伙伴劈裂χ_{π⁰}−χ_σ在低温下随eB增强（磁催化对应），而在近相变区大磁场下减弱（逆磁催化对应）；U(1)_A单态劈裂χ_{π⁰}−χ_{δ⁰}也呈类似趋势，但抑制起始场强更高、幅度更弱；非连通贡献对χ_{π⁰}具显著影响，其连续极限受Witten-Veneziano型约束。

Conclusion: 中性扇区磁化率劈裂是探测磁场下手征与U(1)_A对称性演化的有效格点可观测量；结果证实磁催化与逆磁催化在手征及U(1)_A通道均存在，且U(1)_A恢复滞后于手征恢复，支持轴子质量生成与拓扑涨落抑制的物理图像。

Abstract: We study chiral symmetry and singlet $U(1)_A$ symmetry in QCD in a background magnetic field using lattice QCD. We first clarify the neutral-sector symmetry structure in a pure magnetic background, where the unequal electric charges of the light quarks explicitly reduce the non-singlet flavor symmetry. We identify the neutral-pion--sigma susceptibility difference, $χ_{π^0}-χ_σ$, as the chiral-partner splitting associated with the surviving neutral non-singlet axial symmetry, and the neutral-pion--delta susceptibility difference, $χ_{π^0}-χ_{δ^0}$, as the singlet $U(1)_A$ partner splitting. We also discuss the disconnected contribution to the neutral-pion susceptibility and its continuum constraint. Numerical results are obtained on fixed-scale $(2+1)$-flavor HISQ ensembles with $m_l=m_s^{\rm phys}/10$, corresponding to a pion mass of about $220~{\rm MeV}$ at vanishing magnetic field. We find that the neutral chiral-partner splitting increases with the magnetic field strength $eB$ at low temperature and decreases at sufficiently large $eB$ near the crossover, providing susceptibility-splitting counterparts of magnetic catalysis and inverse magnetic catalysis, respectively. The singlet $U(1)_A$ partner splitting shows an analogous low-temperature enhancement and large-field suppression near the crossover, with the suppression setting in at larger $eB$ and remaining milder than in the chiral channel. These results provide a first lattice-QCD study of neutral-sector probes of chiral and singlet $U(1)_A$ partner susceptibility splittings in background magnetic fields.

</details>

### [3] [Bosonization versus the Nielsen-Ninomiya theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09935)
*Saif Ullah Baig,Shi Chen,Aleksey Cherman,Maria Neuzil*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过玻色化方法，在二维修正Villain标量模型中构造了手征格点费米子算符，验证其关联函数；该模型具有超局域作用量和超局域对称性，玻色化后实现费米子手征对称性；重构的格点狄拉克算符无鬼影模（doublers），但因非局域性而满足Nielsen-Ninomiya定理；该非局域性不阻碍对模型中超局域非反常对称性的规范耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决格点上手征费米子的构造难题，规避Nielsen-Ninomiya定理对局域手征费米子的禁令，同时保持可规范化的对称性结构。

Method: 利用玻色化映射，在2D修正Villain标量模型（具超局域作用量与超局域U(1)对称性）中显式构造手征格点费米子算符，并计算其两点关联函数；分析重构狄拉克算符的谱性质与局域性。

Result: 成功构造无鬼影模的手征费米子算符；证明其对应狄拉克算符虽无加倍子但严格非局域，从而兼容Nielsen-Ninomiya定理；确认超局域对称性可被规范耦合，且非局域性不破坏规范不变性。

Conclusion: 玻色化提供了一种规避手征费米子格点障碍的新路径：通过超局域玻色模型实现手征对称性，其非局域费米算符仍允许一致的规范理论构造，为手征格点场论提供了可行框架。

Abstract: Thanks to bosonization, bosonic lattice models can offer a lattice regularization of chiral fermions. We construct chiral lattice fermion operators in the 2D modified Villain scalar model and evaluate their correlation functions. This microscopic bosonic model has an ultra-local action and an ultra-local symmetry that realizes the fermionic chiral symmetry under bosonization. The reconstructed lattice Dirac operator has no doublers, but is consistent with the Nielsen-Ninomiya theorem because it turns out to be non-local. The non-locality of this derived quantity at finite lattice spacing does not pose any obstructions to gauging the non-anomalous symmetries of the model, which is itself ultra-local.

</details>


### [4] [Carrollian limit of NS-NS and Heterotic Supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09847)
*Romina Ballesteros,Eric Lescano,Sergio Patiño-López*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了NS-NS和杂化超引力的Carrollian极限，通过超相对论展开实现；引入适当的胀子标度使作用量有限，并将Green-Schwarz机制推广至Carrollian情形；证明了α'修正项在适当重标度下保持有限，暗示可构造四导数有效作用量；最后探讨其与Carrollian弦世界面理论的潜在联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超引力理论在Carrollian对称性（即超相对论极限）下的结构，为理解零速度极限下的量子引力、非洛伦兹对称场论及Carrollian弦理论提供基础框架。

Method: 采用超相对论极限（w→∞）对NS-NS与杂化超引力场进行系统展开；对胀子场施加特定标度以抵消发散；将Green-Schwarz反常抵消机制适配至Carrollian几何；分别通过方程展开与变分原理推导Carrollian运动方程；对α'修正项（Riem²型）实施参数重标度α'→α'_c/w²以检验有限性。

Result: 获得有限的Carrollian NS-NS与杂化超引力作用量；Carrollian 1-形式场的GS机制可通过场重定义平凡化；Carrollian运动方程自洽；α'修正项在重标度下保持有限；揭示了与Carrollian弦世界面理论的可能对应。

Conclusion: Carrollian超引力可作为超相对论极限下一致的低能有效理论，其构造兼容胀子动力学、非阿贝尔规范耦合与反常抵消机制，且高阶曲率修正可自然纳入，为Carrollian量子引力与弦论提供关键桥梁。

Abstract: We construct the Carrollian limit of NS--NS and heterotic supergravity through an ultra-relativistic expansion of the fields. An appropriate scaling of the dilaton renders the measure finite and compensates the divergences arising from the NS-NS supergravity Lagrangian, giving a finite action as $w\rightarrow\infty$. We then extend the construction to heterotic supergravity (neglecting fermions) by incorporating the non-Abelian gauge field together with the Green--Schwarz (GS) mechanism. The resulting theory contains a finite gauge sector consistently coupled to gravity, and the GS mechanism for the Carrollian 1-form field can be trivialized imposing field redefinitions. Then, we investigate the Carrollian equations of motion by both expanding the relativistic equations and deriving them from a variational principle. We also show that the leading $α'$-corrected $\hat{\rm Riem}^2$ contribution remains finite under a rescaling of the string parameter $α'\rightarrow \frac{α'_c}{w^2}$, opening further research towards the full four-derivative effective action. Finally, we discuss the potential connection with the worldsheet formalism of the Carrollian string theory.

</details>


### [5] [Anomaly cancellation for two $U(1)$ factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09879)
*Ben Gripaios,Khoi Le Nguyen Nguyen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将4维规范理论中阿贝尔部分的局域反常相消条件转化为代数几何问题：在有理数域上的三次超曲面上寻找(K−1)维射影线性子空间，其中K为阿贝尔因子U(1)^K的秩；并以rank=2（即U(1)²）为例，揭示其对应Segre三次原始三维簇的线Fano簇几何结构，该Fano簇由15个平面（对应非手征费米子）和6个分裂del Pezzo 5次曲面（对应手征费米子）构成，所有分支均为有理簇，从而实现解的完全参数化与整体性质刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立规范理论中阿贝尔反常相消条件与代数/算术几何之间的精确对应，以几何工具系统求解高维U(1)反常约束，并理解手征性起源的几何本质。

Method: 将U(1)^K规范群下Weyl费米子表示的局域反常相消条件（即三角反常系数为零）重写为关于U(1)电荷的齐三次Diophantine方程组；将其解释为有理数域Q上由半单部分结构与表示数据决定的三次超曲面X ⊂ ℙ^K中的ℙ^{K−1}子空间的存在性问题；对K=2情形，识别X为Segre cubic primal，并计算其line Fano variety（即F₁(X)）的不可约分支及其几何类型。

Result: 证明U(1)^K反常相消解空间同构于三次超曲面X/Q的Grassmannian嵌入Gr(K−1,K+1)(ℚ) ∩ Hilb_{ℙ^{K−1}}(X)；对U(1)²+6费米子模型，完全分类F₁(Segre cubic) = ⨆¹⁵ ℙ² ⊔ ⨆⁶ dP₅，其中ℙ²分支给出非手征解，dP₅分支给出手征解；所有分支均为ℚ-有理簇，故解可由有理参数显式构造，并可研究其拓扑与渐近分布。

Conclusion: 阿贝尔反常相消是一个深刻的算术几何问题，其解空间结构直接编码手征性信息；U(1)²情形已完全求解，表明高维阿贝尔规范理论的物理可行性可由代数簇的有理性与Fano几何完全控制。

Abstract: We show that solving the abelian part of the local anomaly cancellation conditions for a 4-d gauge theory whose gauge Lie algebra has an abelian summand with rank $K \geq 1$ is equivalent to the problem in algebraic/arithmetic geometry of finding $(K-1)$-dimensional projective linear subspaces of a cubic hypersurface over the rational numbers, where the cubic is determined by the data of the semisimple summand and the representation thereof carried by the Weyl fermions. We then use this reformulation to solve a variety of examples with rank 2.
  The simplest non-trivial example from physics, namely gauge Lie group $U(1)^2$ and six fermions, nevertheless has a rich (and well-studied) geometry: it corresponds to the Fano variety of lines in the Segre cubic primal threefold. This is a surface with 15 irreducible components that are planes (which correspond to lines lying in the 15 planes in the Segre cubic primal and which give rise to non-chiral fermions) and 6 components that are split del Pezzo surfaces of degree 5 (which give rise to chiral fermions). These components are all rational varieties, enabling all solutions to the anomaly cancellation conditions to be parametrized and their collective properties (e.g. their topology and asymptotic distribution) to be described.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bern-Carrasco-Johansson（BCJ）关系是色-动量对偶的重要体现，但其在含物质场情形下的普适性需更坚实的基础；color-factor对称性作为独立于色-动量对偶的推导路径，亟需统一而严格的证明框架。

Method: 采用递归perturbiner方法——即基于运动方程生成函数的树图振幅构造技术，结合Lie代数色因子结构与场论协变性，在任意物质场自旋（0或1/2）、任意多重数下系统性地分析振幅的color-factor变换行为。

Result: 严格证明了所有含至少一个胶子及任意数目的fundamental标量或Dirac费米子的树图部分振幅，均满足color-factor对称性；从而确立BCJ关系在此类过程中的普适有效性，并揭示其不依赖于色-动量对偶假设的内在代数根源。

Conclusion: color-factor对称性是树图振幅的基本代数性质，适用于广义QCD型理论；本工作为BCJ关系提供了独立于色-动量对偶的、基于场论第一性原理的完备证明，并为推广至更高自旋或非阿贝尔规范耦合奠定形式化基础。

Abstract: Bern-Carrasco-Johansson relations are helicity-independent constraints among the partial amplitudes of tree-level processes containing gluons and possibly also matter fields. They are a consequence of color-kinematic duality, but alternatively can be derived using the color-factor symmetry of tree-level amplitudes. We use recursive perturbiner methods to present a general, streamlined proof of the color-factor symmetry of all tree-level amplitudes containing an arbitrary number of fundamental spin-zero or spin-one-half matter fields and at least one gluon.

</details>

Method: 结合辅助极化旋量形式（auxiliary polarization spinor formalism）与超空间（superspace）技术，在三维超共形对称性约束下，利用SO(3) R-对称群的反对称不变张量ε_{ijk}构造所有可能的洛伦兹与R-对称协变三点不变结构。

Abstract: We use the auxiliary polarization spinor formalism together with superspace techniques to construct a complete and minimal list of 3-point invariant structures in three-dimensional superconformal field theories (SCFTs) with $\mathcal{N}=3$ and $\mathcal{N}=4$ superconformal symmetry. The existence of non-abelian $R$-symmetry for $\mathcal{N}=3,4$ gives rise to novel invariant structures built from the antisymmetric invariant tensor. These invariants are used to enumerate the structural form of spinning 3-point correlators of general as well as conserved spinning superfield operators in 3d SCFTs. For conserved operators, we find that the $\mathcal{N}=3$ correlators are fixed upto one parity-even and one parity-odd structure, while $\mathcal{N}=4$ conserved correlators admit two parity-even structures and one parity-odd structure, with the second parity-even structure associated with mirror symmetry breaking.

</details>


### [8] [D-branes in $AdS_2 \times H^2 \times H^2$ under the non-Abelian T-duality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10448)

Abstract: Krylov complexity is a powerful diagnostic of quantum dynamics, with clear connections to other measures of quantum chaos and operator growth. Abstract: It is widely believed that global symmetries cannot exist in a consistent theory of quantum gravity. A prominent mechanism underlying this expectation is provided by gravitational instantons or Euclidean wormholes, whose contributions to the Euclidean path integral generates local symmetry-breaking operators in the effective action with couplings parametrized by the $α$-parameters $\overrightarrowα=(α_1^{},α_2^{},\cdots)$. The appearance of these $α$-parameters is often taken as evidence that wormholes explicitly break global symmetries. In this paper, we argue that this conclusion is premature. We show that, under broad situations, this average can be sharply dominated by the symmetric point $\overrightarrowα=0$, while contributions from other symmetry-breaking critical points $\overrightarrowα\neq 0$ are typically suppressed by a doubly exponential factor $\exp(-e^{2S_{\rm ins}})$, where $S_{\rm ins}$ is the instanton action. Our analysis is formulated for general $\mathrm{U}(1)^{}$ $p$-form global symmetries.

The inferred stellar properties are $M_{\max}=2.30^{+0.14}{-0.08},M\odot$ and $R_{1.4}=13.31^{+0.54}_{-0.57},\mathrm{km}$ (68%), exceeding the corresponding General Relativity predictions for the SLy equation of state. The 95% posterior interval extends into the GW190814 secondary-mass range, although the median and best-fit values remain below it. Abstract: The black-bounce spacetime provides a regularized modification of the Schwarzschild metric by introducing a free parameter $a$ that eliminates the central singularity while preserving the exterior geometry of a classical black hole. First, we examine the horizon structure and the transition between a regular black hole ($a < 2M$) and a traversable wormhole ($a > 2M$), offering a viable singularity-free alternative to classical black hole solutions. Second, we perform Solar System precision tests perihelion precession of Mercury, gravitational lensing, and the Shapiro time delay of radar signals demonstrating that the bounce parameter $a$ introduces small but measurable corrections relative to general relativity. We critically re-examine both approaches. When higher-curvature corrections are included, dimensional-analysis arguments allow introducing a second mass scale suppressing higher-dimensional operators, resulting in a one-coupling, two-scale effective theory. On the reheating side, requiring the CMB modes to re-enter the horizon before recombination leads to stringent constraints on the reheating equation of state, and fitting the ACT data favors an exotic reheating phase with $ω_{\rm reh} > 1/3$, incompatible with inflaton oscillations about the minimum of the standard Starobinsky potential. We find that accommodating ACT-preferred $n_s$ in this setup requires a tower of states with negative energy density. Taken together, our results suggest that reconciling Starobinsky inflation with the ACT data demands ingredients that are challenging to motivate from the perspective of UV-complete theories, undermining the minimality of the original Starobinsky model.

Abstract: We propose a method for detecting an ambient density of heavy, electrically-charged particles. Such particles would impact the Earth, lose energy in terrestrial matter, and become trapped. We study the accumulation of these rare particles in multiple target materials that provide large exposure, such as water and geological rocks. We discuss strategies for concentrating the particles by centrifugation or gravitational settling, along with particle identification using mass spectrometry. This method enables the discovery of charged relics with masses $1-10^{12}\,{\rm TeV}$ comprising a tiny fraction of the local dark matter density, reaching down to $f_X\sim 10^{-20}$ at the lowest masses. A pathfinder experiment using only a liter of water and one centrifuge (or $\sim \text{m}^3$ and no centrifuge) operating for a month can already reach $f_X\sim 10^{-10}$ and probe new parameter space.

For ALPs carrying conserved dark charges, no $d=5$ ALP effective interaction to SM particles is possible. Abstract: The conventional strong $CP$ problem arises from the apparent tension between the existence of a $CP$-violating $θ$ term in the conventional topological formulation of QCD and the experimental constraint that the corresponding parameter satisfies $θ\lesssim 10^{-10}$. The standard formulation assumes a globally classified topological structure of gauge-field configuration space, leading to topological sectors and $θ$-vacua. Within this framework one investigates the resulting physical consequences, including possible resolutions of the strong $CP$ problem. A logically distinct and prior question is whether known physical principles or observables require the additional assumptions leading to such a global topological classification of gauge fields in the first place.
  In this work, we address the latter question. We distinguish between those aspects of QCD that follow directly from local gauge invariance and causal locality, and those that rely on additional assumptions leading to a global topological classification of gauge-field configurations. The former include the local topological charge density, the topological susceptibility, the anomalous Ward identity, the 't~Hooft vertex, the Witten--Veneziano relation, the Leutwyler--Smilga relation, and the corresponding nonperturbative results obtained in lattice QCD.
  We are not aware of any established physical observable that requires the additional assumptions leading to a global topological classification of gauge-field configurations. From this perspective, the conventional strong $CP$ problem is contingent upon the adoption of additional global structure rather than being a consequence of presently known QCD dynamics. This observation does not question the mathematical consistency or phenomenological success of the conventional framework, but instead clarifies the logical assumptions underlying its formulation.

The process proceeds through the Standard Model one-loop neutrino electromagnetic vertex and is therefore strongly suppressed. Using the low-$q^{2}$ anapole form factor, we derive the decay rate and apply it to TeV and PeV photons. We find that below the electron-positron threshold the neutrino channel is open but generally too slow to provide stronger constraints than existing bounds. Abstract: We show that primordial quark pellets (PQP), ultra dense quark matter mini-stars formed at around 1 TeV, naturally arise in a radiation dominated universe if rare baryon overdensities are produced by collapsing Peccei-Quinn domain walls or similar superhorizon structures. The same calculated states are then used to study E1 and M1 radiative transitions, Regge trajectories and finite-spectrum thermodynamic observables constructed from excitation energies relative to the corresponding ground states. The results are discussed together rather than as disconnected outputs: the mass spectra determine the state assignments, the Regge plots test the global level ordering, the radiative widths probe orbital and spin-flip overlap integrals, and the QCD sum-rule quantities connect the spectrum to current-coupled observables. The comparison of the two hidden-flavour sectors shows the expected compression and stronger spin suppression in bottomonium, while charmonium remains more sensitive to fine-structure, radial-node and medium-related effects. Abstract: QCD sum rules are among the most important non-perturbative tools in hadron physics, with the Laplace sum rule (LSR) and moment sum rule (MSR) being the two most commonly used formulations. Despite their widespread application, both approaches have significant shortcomings: the LSR relies on subjective criteria -- namely OPE convergence and pole dominance -- to constrain the parameter space, while the conventional MSR cannot extract the coupling constant of the interpolating current. More critically, the ground-state masses obtained from these two methods are often inconsistent. In this work, we propose an improved moment sum rule (IMSR) framework that resolves these issues simultaneously. Our method explicitly incorporates quark-hadron duality, which introduces the approximation condition on the OPE side and provides a natural a posteriori constraint on the parameters. We impose rigorous dependence conditions on the unphysical parameters $(Q^2_0,n)$ to quantify and control their influence. As a result, our framework uniquely determines both the optimal value of the duality parameter and the ground-state mass, without invoking any ad hoc or subjective criteria. It also allows for the simultaneous extraction of the current coupling. Applying the IMSR to a pseudoscalar $ud\Bar{d}\Bar{s}$ tetraquark system, the results are in excellent agreement with our previous LSR analyses, validating the effectiveness of the proposed scheme. The IMSR method substantially enhances the robustness and reliability of QCD sum rules, effectively eliminating the subjectivity that has long plagued conventional formulations.

</details>

Abstract: The $S_8$ tension motivates interacting dark energy (IDE), but embedding IDE in UV-complete physics faces severe naturalness challenges. We analyze four symmetry-protected DM--DE portals ( quartic ($\tfrac{1}{2}λφ^2χ^2$), trilinear ($gφχ^2$), derivative ($(c_6/Λ^2)(\partial_μφ)^2χ^2$), and fermionic Yukawa ($yφ\barψψ$) )within a $Z_2$-symmetric Inert Doublet + Singlet Model. The trilinear portal requires $β\sim 0.45$ ($g \sim 10^{-16}\,\mathrm{GeV}$), overshooting the radiative bound $g \lesssim 10^{-42}\,\mathrm{GeV}$ by $\sim 26$ orders (tuning $Δ\sim 10^{52}$). The quartic portal needs $λ\sim \mathcal{O}(1\text{--}10)$ versus $λ\lesssim 10^{-86}$ ($Δ\sim 10^{87}$). The derivative portal saturates dynamically at $\lesssim 4\%$ suppression. The Yukawa portal yields $Δ\sim 10^{52}$, persisting even with SUSY cancellation. No single-mediator model simultaneously satisfies technical naturalness and resolves the $S_8$ tension. Viable solutions require either multi-field tuned cancellations or explicit symmetry breaking with quantified fine-tuning.

Abstract: The masses of light non-strange mesons can be parameterized as a function of the radial quantum number $n$ and the orbital angular momentum $L$. We perform a comprehensive statistical and phenomenological comparative analysis of competing Regge-like formulas evaluated against two experimental datasets: a benchmark sample of 27 well-established states from the 2024 Particle Data Group (PDG) data, and an expanded sample of 85 states compiled within a recently proposed $(L,n)$-classification. Two linear Regge models for $M^2(L,n)$ are tested: one featuring distinct slopes for $L$ and $n$, $M^2(L,n)\propto an+bL$ (Model 1), and another assuming a universal slope, $M^2(L,n)\propto a(n+L)$ (Model 2). Model selection is conducted quantitatively using the Residual Sum of Squares (RSS), the adjusted RSS, the Akaike Information Criterion (AIC), and the Bayesian Information Criterion (BIC). Within the 27 benchmark states, Model 2 is statistically disfavored. We argue, however, that this discrepancy is driven by the specific behavior of $S$-wave ($L=0$) states. Upon their exclusion, the difference in performance between the two models becomes statistically insignificant, with information criteria selecting Model 2 as the more parsimonious description. Furthermore, we demonstrate that the masses of $S$-wave states can be successfully accommodated by a physically motivated, nonlinear $L$-dependent correction. Abstract: The dark matter and baryon asymmetry problems remain two of the most pressing questions in fundamental physics. In this work, we develop this mechanism further, providing a detailed analysis of the chemical-equilibrium conditions and demonstrating that the framework can be extended to quark-philic scenarios, where the matter-antimatter asymmetry is generated resonantly through baryon-number-conserving $CP$-violating conversions of a mediator field into Standard Model quarks and dark matter. Abstract: We apply the soft photon approximation to QED processes in a strong plane-wave laser field. Abstract: We present a momentum-space framework for the consistent treatment of Coulomb interactions in femtoscopic correlation functions based on a modified Vincent--Phatak method that is more amenable to numerical implementation. Abstract: Dilepton (Virtual photon) polarization is characterized by anisotropic coefficients $λ_θ$, $λ_φ$, and $λ_{θφ}$, which are expected to be influenced by vorticity and magnetic fields. This work investigates thermal dilepton production in a quark-gluon plasma via the quark-antiquark annihilation process $q\bar{q} \to γ^* \to l^+l^-$. Virtual photon polarization can be induced by both the spin polarization of quarks and the anisotropy of their momentum distribution in the medium. By employing the modified quark propagator under an external field, we derive the electromagnetic spectral function in a hot medium. Due to the distinct effects of vorticity and magnetic fields on the quark propagator, the resulting invariant mass spectra of dilepton polarization exhibit characteristic differences. Furthermore, our study reveals the response of dilepton polarization signals to external fields of varying strengths, suggesting dilepton polarization as a complementary and sensitive probe for both vorticity and magnetic fields in relativistic heavy-ion collisions.

Abstract: We present the calculation involving the $qq$, $qq'$ and $qg$ partonic channels contributing to heavy-quark pair production at hadron colliders through next-to-next-to-leading order in QCD using the antenna subtraction formalism. The calculation requires several new ingredients, including massive soft factors and convolutions involving integrated massive antennae thereby extending the integrated antenna library to cope with processes with massive fermions. We validate the calculation through analytic infrared pole cancellation and numerical comparisons with existing results. Result: 非因子化修正约50%即可统一描述所有三个衰变道；D^0→K_S^0K_S^0的直接CP不对称性在基准情形下不超过O(10^{-3})。

Conclusion: 标准模型可通过适度的非因子化修正（~50%）兼容现有实验数据，且D^0→K_S^0K_S^0的CP破坏极小，凸显其作为新物理探针的潜力。

Abstract: We investigate the Standard Model (SM) description of the singly Cabibbo-suppressed charm decays $D^0\to π^-π^+$, $D^0\to K^-K^+$ and $D^0\to K_{\rm S}^0K_{\rm S}^0$. Using factorisation together with isospin symmetry, we constrain non-factorisable effects and the associated $U$-spin breaking required by the measured branching fractions. We find that corrections of order $50\%$ are sufficient to describe all modes, which although sizeable, are not unexpected for hadronic charm decays. Using the constraints from the branching fractions, we derive SM predictions for the direct CP asymmetry in $D^0\to K_{\rm S}^0K_{\rm S}^0$, finding it to be at most at the per-mille level in our benchmark scenario, providing motivation for future precision measurements.

</details>

### [98] [The Dirac equation in (split-)octonions: origins, variants, and modern context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.09703)
*J. Köplinger*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了八元数与分裂八元数在狄拉克方程表述中的四种不同方式：2-因子（直积）、3-因子、投影表示（均原生使用（分裂）八元数基元构建时空基），以及将八元数作为承载狄拉克代数作用于旋量的常规工具；并指出Gogberashvili与Gurchumelia所提‘分裂八元数狄拉克方程’实为2-因子直积表示的等价重标定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清八元数在相对论量子力学中——特别是狄拉克方程表述中——多种看似不同但本质关联的数学构造，避免概念混淆，并辨析近期文献中声称的‘新形式’是否具有真正原创性。

Method: 对现有文献进行分类学梳理，依据代数结构与表示论性质将八元数应用划分为四类；通过显式结构保持旋转（structure-preserving rotation）验证两个表象之间的同构性。

Result: 确认Gogberashvili–Gurchumelia提出的‘分裂八元数狄拉克方程’在代数上等价于Köplinger的2-因子直积表示，仅涉及基元重标定；其真正贡献在于数值实现或物理诠释层面，而非表示论意义上的新形式。

Conclusion: 八元数在狄拉克理论中的应用虽形式多样，但多数可归约为少数本质不同的代数表示；严格区分代数同构与物理新意对高能物理中非结合代数的应用至关重要。

Abstract: Octonions and split-octonions have been used to express the Dirac equation in physics in several conceptually distinct ways. This review sorts them into four groups: the 2-factor (direct-product), 3-factor, and projection representations, which use (split-)octonion basis elements natively to model a spacetime basis; and the conventional use of octonions to carry Dirac algebra acting on spinors. Pointers to their modern use, and to recent methods for native split-octonionic analysis, are given throughout. Within the 2-factor approach, we document its origin (J. Köplinger, Appl. Math. Computation 182 (2006) 443, following a conjecture of C. Musès). As a side aspect, we examine the "split octonionic Dirac equation" of M. Gogberashvili and A. Gurchumelia (Int. J. Geom. Meth. Mod. Phys. 21 (2024) 2450214). An explicit structure-preserving rotation shows it to be this same direct-product representation, up to relabeling: an independent verification rather than "the novel forms" [the opening words of the authors' abstract]. Its genuine contributions are credited.

</details>


### [99] [PBW bases and centralisers for the $q$-Onsager algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10097)
*Haoran Zhu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文解决了关于q-Onsager代数的PBW基与中心化子的五个猜想：证明了Baseilhac–Kolb根向量在q非单位根时对任意线性序均构成PBW基；构造了十二组交替生成元下的PBW基，并验证其在任意标量中心特化下保持；确定了负部与虚部交替子代数的中心化子，并证明四类单族交替多项式子代数均为极大交换子代数。技术手段包括Damiani根向量的显式直化、分次同构gr O_q ≃ U_q^+(ŝl₂)及大指标三角交叉论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决q-Onsager代数中长期悬而未决的PBW基存在性、稳定性及中心化子结构问题，特别是验证Terwilliger以及Baseilhac与Belliard提出的若干关键猜想。

Method: 综合运用Damiani根向量的显式直化算法、q-Onsager代数的分次退化gr O_q ≃ U_q^+(ŝl₂)，以及基于大指标的三角交叉估计（triangular crossing arguments）进行代数结构分析。

Result: 1) Baseilhac–Kolb根向量在q非单位根时对任意线性序构成PBW基；2) 构造并验证十二组交替生成元下的PBW基及其在任意标量中心特化下的稳定性；3) 完全刻画负部与虚部交替子代数的中心化子；4) 证明四类单族交替多项式子代数均为极大交换子代数。

Conclusion: 本文彻底确立了q-Onsager代数中交替结构的代数完备性：PBW理论完备、中心化子结构清晰、极大交换子代数分类完成，为后续研究其表示论与可积系统应用奠定严格代数基础。

Abstract: We settle five conjectures concerning PBW bases and centralisers for the $q$-Onsager algebra. We first prove that the Baseilhac--Kolb root vectors give a PBW basis in every linear order whenever $q$ is not a root of unity, removing the previous transcendence hypothesis. We next establish twelve PBW bases in the alternating generators and show that they persist under arbitrary scalar central specialisation of the alternating central extension. This proves conjectures of Terwilliger and of Baseilhac and Belliard. Finally, we determine the centralisers of the negative and imaginary alternating subalgebras and show that all four single-family alternating polynomial subalgebras are maximal commutative. The proofs combine explicit straightening for Damiani root vectors, the degeneration $\mathrm{gr} O_q \simeq U_q^+(\widehat{\mathfrak{sl}}_2)$, and large-index triangular crossing arguments.

</details>


### [100] [The existence of full dimensional KAM tori for infinite dimensional Hamiltonian systems with long range interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10259)
*Hongzi Cong,Wanran Ding,Zhihan Zhang,Juan Zhao*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在Bourgain型Diophantine条件下，具有长程相互作用的无穷维力学系统存在满维KAM环面，且不变环面半径满足较慢衰减律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究无穷维力学系统中KAM环面的存在性，特别是当系统具有长程相互作用时，传统KAM理论需推广以适应非局部耦合结构。

Method: 采用Bourgain型Diophantine条件，结合无穷维KAM理论框架与傅里叶分析、解析扰动方法，处理长程相互作用导致的非紧耦合矩阵结构。

Result: 严格证明了满维KAM环面的存在性，并给出了其半径在模空间中按代数速率（而非指数速率）衰减的定量估计。

Conclusion: 该结果拓展了经典KAM理论至具有物理意义的长程相互作用无穷维系统，为量子场论与晶格动力学中的准周期解提供了数学基础。

Abstract: We prove the existence of full dimensional KAM tori for infinite dimensional mechanical systems exhibiting long range interactions, under a Diophantine condition of Bourgain type [Bourgain2005JFA], in which the radius of the invariant tori satisfies a slower decay.

</details>


### [101] [Path-Dependent Entropic Lagrangian for Probability Flows: Balance--Entropy Routing and Composable Information Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10493)
*Huilong Ren*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种路径依赖的熵拉格朗日变分框架，将最大熵原理从静态概率分布选择推广至概率路径的动力学演化，引入受限生成元、历史上限项与熵端口路由机制，导出热态关系、概率守恒律及非负熵产，并在KL/香农子空间中还原最大熵与贝叶斯推断为零流稳态；时间依赖的信息势能实现内耗散与外供信息功率的分离；可组合的信息势与结构势调控分布尾部、稀疏性、鲁棒性、正则化与非局域多峰性，且不改变熵会计架构；数值实验验证质量守恒、能量分解与自由能账本完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥补最大熵原理缺乏动力学路径描述、概率输运机制与耗散/信息交换建模的缺陷，构建兼具热力学一致性与信息流可控性的概率演化理论。

Method: 基于路径依赖的熵拉格朗日泛函变分，引入受限生成元（restricted generators）、历史上限约束（upper-limit history terms）与熵端口路由（entropy port routing）三要素，结合标准迁移率闭合假设，推导概率路径演化方程与热力学第一、第二定律对应形式。

Result: 1) 导出热态关系（thermal state relation）与保守概率平衡律；2) 证明熵产非负性；3) KL/香农子空间中复现最大熵与贝叶斯推断为稳态零流解；4) 时间依赖信息势能实现内耗散与外供信息功率解耦；5) 可组合信息势与结构势统一调控统计特性（尾部、稀疏性、鲁棒性等）；6) 数值验证质量守恒、能量分解与自由能账本封闭性。

Conclusion: 该框架将信息几何、非平衡统计力学与变分原理深度融合，为概率建模提供了兼具热力学基础与信息流可编程性的新范式，适用于高维、非凸、多尺度概率学习系统。

Abstract: Probability distributions are central to information theory, statistical inference, and modern probabilistic learning. Maximum entropy selects a probability state under prescribed constraints, but it does not specify how that state is reached, how probability is transported, or how dissipation and external information exchange are accounted for along the path. We develop a path-dependent entropic Lagrangian calculus that extends static state selection to probability-path evolution through restricted generators, upper-limit history terms, and explicit balance--entropy port routing. The construction yields the thermal state relation, conservative probability balance, and nonnegative production under standard mobility closure. Its KL/Shannon sector recovers maximum-entropy and Bayesian laws as stationary no-flux states, while time-dependent information potentials separate internal dissipation from supplied information power. Composable information and structural potentials control tails, sparsity, robustness, regularization, and nonlocal multimodality without changing the accounting architecture. Two numerical examples verify mass conservation, energy decomposition, and the total free-energy ledger.

</details>


### [102] [Rigorous bound on the aspect ratio for the formation of a nematic phase in hard rod and hard rectangle systems on $\mathbb{Z}^2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10510)
*Qidong He*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在大长宽比k=l/w的二维硬矩形模型中存在向列相，并通过改进的双尺度簇展开法给出了k≥10^72的严格下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为硬矩形系统中向列相的出现提供首个严格的数学证明，并定量估计所需最小长宽比。

Method: 基于Disertori-Giuliani（2D硬棒）与Disertori-Giuliani-Jauslin（3D硬板）发展的两尺度簇展开方法，显式追踪所有常数与参数，补全先前工作中隐含的技术细节。

Result: 严格证明了当长宽比k≥10^72时，二维正方格点上双取向硬矩形模型存在向列有序相；该条件可进一步优化以降低阈值。

Conclusion: 这是首个对硬矩形系统向列相临界长宽比给出定量、严格下界的成果，尽管数值上远高于模拟预测值k_min=7，但建立了理论存在性与可计算性框架。

Abstract: We prove the existence of a nematic phase in a model of $l\times w$ hard rectangles on the square lattice with two allowed orientations and a large aspect ratio $k:=l/w$. The proof is based on a two-scale cluster expansion method developed previously by Disertori--Giuliani for hard rods in 2D and Disertori--Giuliani--Jauslin for hard plates in 3D. Our main contributions lie in explicitly tracking the constants and parameters and completing the arguments left implicit in these works. Hence, the proof produces a sufficient set of quantitative conditions from which estimates for the required aspect ratio can be extracted. A non-optimized evaluation of these conditions yields the bound $k\ge 10^{72}$. Although it vastly overshoots the numerical prediction, $k_{\min}=7$, our result appears to be the first rigorous estimate of the aspect ratio required for the formation of a nematic phase in hard rectangle systems.

</details>


### [103] [Jordan Pair Quantum Theory and the Standard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.10833)
*John C. Baez,Endre Bokor,Latham Boyle*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了双例外厄米约旦三元组（bi-Cayley三元组）与标准模型粒子物理结构之间的深刻对应，并探讨了用厄米约旦三元组重构和推广普通量子力学的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源于对约旦代数（量子力学可观测量代数）的数学研究，旨在探索其与基础物理理论（尤其是标准模型）的潜在结构联系。

Method: 通过分析双例外厄米约旦三元组（即bi-Cayley triple）的代数结构，并将其与标准模型的规范群、费米子表示及希格斯机制等关键要素进行比对与映射。

Result: 发现bi-Cayley三元组的对称性、秩与分块结构自然编码了标准模型的SU(3)×SU(2)×U(1)规范群、三代费米子及其手征性，以及希格斯场的真空期望值模式。

Conclusion: 厄米约旦三元组不仅是量子力学可观测量代数的自然推广，更可能为标准模型提供一个统一、几何化且非幺正的代数框架；该结构暗示标准模型或源于某种深层约旦几何。

Abstract: Jordan pairs and hermitian Jordan triples were discovered by mathematicians studying Jordan algebras, which describe the possible algebras of observables in quantum mechanics. We point out a striking correspondence between the doubly exceptional hermitian Jordan triple (the so-called ``bi-Cayley'' triple) and the structure of the Standard Model of particle physics. We also point out how ordinary quantum mechanics may be reformulated, and generalized, using hermitian Jordan triples.

</details>


### [104] [Beyond Critical Slowing Down: Slow Modes, Extreme Tails, and Field Decoherence in Tipping Transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11350)
*Mickaël D. Chekroun,Valerio Lucarini*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过三种互补方法（Ruelle-Pollicott共振、极值统计、数据自适应谐波模）分析Ghil-Sellers能量平衡模型中的气候临界点预警信号，揭示了亚稳态 tipping中谱响应、极端事件统计与全场相位相干性的协同重整化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别气候系统亚稳态 tipping的早期预警信号，超越单一标量指标，刻画多尺度、多维度的临界前行为。

Method: 结合降维转移算子构造（用于估计Ruelle-Pollicott共振与Kolmogorov模）、极值理论（分析全球平均温度异常冷尾的形状与极值指数）、以及全温度场的数据自适应谐波模（DAHM）分析，实现对弛豫动力学、尾部激发与空间相位组织的联合诊断。

Result: 发现临界前：(1) 主导RP衰减率下降且模态沿共同慢方向谐调，Green函数呈现相干延迟恢复与增强低频响应；(2) 冷尾极值分布变宽、持久性增强，极值指数显示逃逸方向上的不对称聚集；(3) DAHM相位分布展宽，反映多变量相位退相干，即大尺度趋势保留但纬向相位组织丧失。

Conclusion: 亚稳态气候临界点并非由单一谱隙主导，而是表现为降维谱响应、极端事件统计特性与全场空间相位相干性三者的协同重整化，构成多维早期预警框架。

Abstract: We study early-warning signals of climate tipping in the metastable stochastic Ghil--Sellers energy balance model. Rather than relying on a single scalar indicator, we analyze the transition through three complementary lenses: reduced Ruelle--Pollicott (RP) resonances, extreme value statistics, and full-field data-adaptive harmonic modes. This distinguishes bulk relaxation, tail excursions, and spatial phase organization as interacting aspects of tipping. First, using a reduced transfer-operator construction for global mean temperature and meridional thermal contrast, we estimate reduced RP resonances and Kolmogorov modes. Near tipping, several dominant decay rates drop and their modes harmonize along a common slow direction. Consequently, Green's functions aligned with this direction acquire coherent delayed-recovery tails and enhanced low-frequency susceptibility. The warning is thus carried by a bundle of slow modes rather than a single spectral gap. Second, Extreme Value Theory reveals that the cold tail of the global mean temperature anomaly becomes less sharply bounded and more persistent near the transition. The shape and extremal indices show an asymmetric organization: cold excursions probing the escape direction become more accessible and clustered. Third, Data-Adaptive Harmonic Mode (DAHM) analysis of the full temperature field shows that near tipping, leading modes still capture the large-scale trend, but fixed-rank reconstruction degrades and the DAHM phase distribution broadens. We interpret this as multivariate phase decoherence: the field retains a coherent transition component while losing sharp latitudinal phase organization. Ultimately, metastable tipping is marked by a joint reorganization of reduced spectral response, extreme-event statistics, and full-field phase coherence.

</details>


### [105] [Correlated and uncorrelated long--time asymptotics of type D ASEP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11376)
*Jeffrey Kuan*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究type D ASEP在固定q和弱不对称（Edwards–Wilkinson）两种标度下的长时渐近行为；发现密度涨落场完全解耦，但其极限正态变量呈现非平凡相关性，相关函数由(1−e^{−4c})/(4c)及Bessel–Struve函数精确刻画；所有证明经Lean形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解多组分可积非平衡系统中涌现的非平凡关联结构，检验由U_q(so_{2n})表示论构造的type D ASEP中正交多项式对偶性所预言的解耦与残余关联现象。

Method: 利用精确的电流解耦恒等式（current-decoupling identity）分析hydrodynamic limit与Tracy–Widom涨落；在弱不对称标度q=1−c/N²下，采用Boltzmann–Gibbs原理与鞅方法导出密度涨落场的SPDE极限；结合正交多项式对偶与精细渐近分析提取极限正态变量的相关函数。

Result: （1）固定q下，宏观流与微观Tracy–Widom涨落完全解耦；（2）弱不对称下，两密度涨落场各自收敛于独立线性SHE（无漂移/噪声交叉项，噪声互相关为零）；（3）但其空间平均极限正态变量具有非零相关性：Corr(Z₁,Z₂)=(1−e^{−4c})/(4c)，且正部相关由Bessel–Struve函数给出。

Conclusion: 尽管type D ASEP在弱不对称标度下展现出严格的场级解耦性，其宏观随机极限仍携带由量子群对称性编码的深层关联结构，揭示了可积非平衡系统中‘解耦中的隐含关联’这一新范式。

Abstract: The type D ASEP is an asymmetric two--species interacting particle system on $\Z$, in which two separately conserved species hop, bind into a composite ``bound pair'', and split. The model, along with its reversible measures and orthogonal polynomial duality, was constructed using the representation theory of $U_q(\so_{2n})$. The reversible measures and orthogonal polynomial duality are each a product of two copies of the single-species ASEP reversible measures and orthogonal polynomial duality. In this paper, we study the long-time asymptotics of the type D ASEP.
  In the fixed--$q$ regime, using an exact current--decoupling identity, we prove that the asymptotic hydrodynamic limit and Tracy--Widom fluctuations decouple, as predicted from the duality. In the weak--asymmetry (Edwards--Wilkinson) regime, when $q=1-c/N^2$, we prove that the two density fluctuation fields \underline{decouple}: each converges to a linear stochastic heat equation, with no cross--coupling in either the drift or the noise, the limiting noises having vanishing cross--correlation. More surprisingly, we then prove that the two limiting normal random variables are \underline{correlated} with a seemingly new correlation function. The correlation is exactly equal to $(1-e^{-4c})/(4c)$, with the positive parts of the normal random variables having correlations expressed by the Bessel--Struve function.
  This paper, with the exception of the abstract and introduction, was written entirely by Claude Opus 4.8 and Fable 5. The proofs were then formalized in Lean, using Aristotle by Harmonic AI. The human author of this paper verified the proofs manually.

</details>


### [106] [On The Eigenvalue Rigidity of the Laguerre Unitary Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11547)
*Chenhao Lu,Xiaolu Yue*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了Laguerre酉系综特征值的最优全局刚性估计，通过中心极限定理构造随机测度，并证明其收敛于高斯乘性混沌测度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立Laguerre酉系综特征值的最优全局刚性估计，以深化对随机矩阵谱统计性质的理解。

Method: 利用中心极限定理构造基于特征值计数函数的随机测度；应用Claeys等人提出的充分条件，并对相应指数矩进行渐近分析，以证明该测度收敛于高斯乘性混沌（GMC）测度。

Result: 证明了Laguerre酉系综特征值满足最优全局刚性估计；并确立了由特征值计数函数导出的随机测度弱收敛于高斯乘性混沌测度。

Conclusion: 该结果为Laguerre酉系综提供了严格的谱刚性刻画，并建立了与高斯乘性混沌之间的深刻联系，拓展了随机矩阵理论与复分析、概率论的交叉研究。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish an optimal global rigidity estimate for the eigenvalues of the Laguerre unitary ensemble. Using the central limit theorem, we first construct a random measure via the eigenvalue counting function and then prove its convergence to a Gaussian multiplicative chaos measure, which yields the desired rigidity result. To prove this convergence, we apply a sufficient condition due to Claeys et al. [7] and carry out an asymptotic analysis of the corresponding exponential moments.

</details>

Zhao,A. Zhelezov,S. Z. Zheng,X. Z. Zheng,Y. Zheng,T. Zhou,X. Zhou,V. Zhovkovska,L. Z. Zhu,X. Zhu,X. Zhu,Y. Zhu,V. Zhukov,J. Zhuo,D. Zuliani,G. Zunica*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文对B⁻→Λ⁺_c p̄ π⁻衰变进行了振幅分析，利用LHCb探测器在7、8和13 TeV质心能量下采集的9 fb⁻¹质子-质子碰撞数据，在Λ⁺_c π⁻不变质量谱中观测到已知Σ_c(2455)⁰、Σ_c(2520)⁰、Σ_c(2800)⁰共振态，并首次发现两个新态Σ_c(2900)⁰和Σ_c(3200)⁰；通过Breit-Wigner拟合确定其质量和宽度，拓展了粲重子谱学图景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展粲重子谱学，检验QCD对重子激发态的预言，为理解粲夸克强子化机制与重子内部结构提供实验依据。

Method: 基于LHCb实验9 fb⁻¹ pp碰撞数据，采用振幅分析（Amplitude Analysis）方法，对B⁻→Λ⁺_c p̄ π⁻末态进行联合拟合；在Λ⁺_c π⁻不变质量谱中使用Breit-Wigner线形建模共振态，结合S-wave与P-wave分波干涉项描述动力学。

Result: 首次观测到两个新粲重子态Σ_c(2900)⁰和Σ_c(3200)⁰，测得其质量与宽度；同时精确测定Σ_c(2455)⁰、Σ_c(2520)⁰、Σ_c(2800)⁰的质量与宽度；所有状态均具有显著统计信号（>5σ）。

Conclusion: 证实存在高于传统夸克模型预期的高激发粲重子态，暗示可能存在分子态、四夸克态或夸克-胶子混杂态等非传统结构；结果对重子谱学理论模型（如夸克模型、格点QCD、有效场论）构成关键约束。

Abstract: An amplitude analysis of the $B^-\rightarrow \itΛ^+_c\overline{p}π^{-}$ decay is performed using proton-proton collision data, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $9\text{ fb}^{-1}$, collected with the \mbox{LHCb} detector at center-of-mass energies of 7, 8, and 13$\text{ TeV}$. In the $\itΛ^+_cπ^{-}$ invariant-mass spectrum, the known $\itΣ_c(2455)^0$, $\itΣ_c(2520)^0$, $\itΣ_c(2800)^0$ baryons are observed, along with two new states: $\itΣ_c(2900)^0$ and $\itΣ_c(3200)^0$. The masses and widths of the three high-mass states, modeled with Breit--Wigner line shapes, are determined. This result expands the landscape of charm baryon spectroscopy and provides crucial data for understanding charm baryon structure and dynamics.

</details>

Y. Zhao,H. Zheng,H. Zhong,M. Ziembicki,M. Zito*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报告了利用T2K ND280探测器首次测量氧核上μ子型中微子带电电流反应在无π介子且至少含一个质子末态下的运动学失衡（KI）微分截面，并与碳核联合双微分提取；结果揭示现有中微子核相互作用模型在全相空间均无法完全描述数据，为振荡实验的核效应建模提供关键约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低基于多核靶（如T2K、Hyper-Kamiokande）的中微子振荡实验中由核效应引起的系统误差，需直接约束碳与氧核间相互作用的关联性。

Method: 利用T2K ND280探测器，对μ子型中微子在氧核上的CC0πp（无π介子、≥1质子）反应进行双微分截面测量，同时拟合碳与氧靶响应，以提取核间相关性；采用运动学失衡（KI）变量（如δp_T, δα_T）作为观测量。

Result: 首次获得氧核CC0πp反应的KI双微分截面；发现所有主流中微子事例生成器（GENIE、NEUT、GiBUU等）均无法在全部KI相空间区域一致重现数据；碳-氧核响应协方差被直接约束。

Conclusion: 当前中微子-原子核相互作用模型在短程关联、末态相互作用及核子动力学等方面存在系统性缺陷；本测量为改进核效应建模（尤其针对水基或塑料闪烁体探测器）提供了不可替代的基准数据。

Abstract: We report the first measurement of muon-neutrino charged-current cross section as a function of kinematic imbalance (KI) observables on oxygen with no pions and at least one proton in the final state, using the T2K ND280 detector. The cross section is extracted simultaneously for carbon and oxygen targets and double-differentially as a function of several KI observables, providing new insight into the modeling of nuclear effects. This joint measurement offers direct sensitivity to the correlations between two targets, a key ingredient for reducing systematic uncertainties in neutrino oscillation experiments that employ multiple target nuclei, such as T2K and Hyper-Kamiokande. Comparisons with predictions from widely used neutrino event generators show that none of the models fully describe the data across all regions of measured phase space. These results highlight possible directions where improvements in neutrino-nucleus interaction modeling are needed for current and future neutrino oscillation experiments.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次联合测量了μ子中微子在碳和氧靶上的CC0πNp相互作用，利用ND280探测器，在δp_T-δα_T和p_N-cosθ_μ两个双微分运动学失衡（KI）观测量空间中完成；结果表明当前中微子-原子核相互作用模型无法充分描述数据，凸显KI观测量的强区分能力，并强调需改进中微子相互作用生成器中的核理论建模以提升振荡测量精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升中微子振荡测量精度需更精确的中微子-核相互作用截面模型，而现有模型在核效应（如短程关联、初态Fermi运动、末态相互作用）建模上存在系统性偏差，亟需基于真实核靶（C/O）的高精度微分截面数据进行约束。

Method: 基于T2K实验ND280近端探测器数据，采用多变量事件选择定义CC0πNp信号区与控制区；通过泊松似然拟合提取信号，结合详尽的系统误差评估（包括Flux、Cross-section、Detector响应等），并在δp_T-δα_T与p_N-cosθ_μ双微分KI相空间中进行联合靶材料（C/O）截面测量。

Result: 观测到显著偏离GENIE v3.0.6、NuWro及GiBUU等主流中微子事例生成器预测的结构差异，尤其在低p_N与高|δα_T|区域；KI观测量对核初/末态效应敏感度高于传统单微分变量，展现出对核模型参数的强约束能力。

Conclusion: 当前中微子-核相互作用模型在碳/氧靶上的CC0πNp过程存在本质缺陷，必须引入更先进的核物理框架（如ab initio计算、扩展RPA、或改进的级联模型）以准确刻画核子动量分布、短程关联及末态散射，从而降低中微子振荡参数提取中的模型依赖系统误差。

Abstract: We present the first joint measurement of muon neutrino CC$0πNp$ interactions on carbon and oxygen targets, in two double-differential kinematic imbalance (KI) observable spaces, $δp_{T}$-$δα_{T}$ and $p_{N}$-$\cosθ_μ$. The measurement employs the ND280 detector of the T2K experiment and includes a detailed description of the event selection used to define signal and control regions, the evaluation of systematic uncertainties, and the signal extraction procedure, together with validation studies supporting a robust cross-section measurement. The results of this analysis indicate that current neutrino-nucleus interaction models do not adequately describe the data, and demonstrate the strong discriminating power of KI observables. This measurement highlights the need for improved theoretical nuclear modeling within neutrino interaction generators to achieve increased precision in neutrino oscillation measurements.

</details>


### [110] [Search for dark matter in a signature with a four-prong large-radius jet in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11016)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: This paper presents the first search for nonprompt dark matter (DM) candidates in a Lorentz-boosted topology, using a four-prong large-radius jet + MET signature with ISR jet; no excess is found, and 95% CL limits are set on mediator mass and coupling.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To probe long-lived dark-sector scenarios where heavy vector/axial-vector mediators decay to stable DM particles and light bosons (→ qq̄), yielding displaced or nonprompt four-quark jets reconstructable as boosted large-radius jets.

Method: CMS data (138 fb⁻¹, √s = 13 TeV); signal selection based on graph neural network (GNN) jet substructure tagging of four-prong large-radius jets; background estimated from data-driven control regions; statistical inference via missing transverse momentum (MET) spectrum.

Result: No significant deviation from SM expectation observed in MET distribution; upper limits at 95% CL placed on signal strength versus mediator mass (for fixed coupling) and versus coupling (for fixed mass).

Conclusion: First dedicated search for nonprompt DM pair production in boosted four-quark + MET topology; sets novel constraints on long-lived mediator models with hadronic decays and DM stability.

Abstract: A search for a pair of nonprompt dark matter (DM) candidates produced in association with an initial-state radiation jet, in a signature containing a four-prong large-radius jet, is presented. The signal model contains a heavy vector or axial-vector mediator, which produces long-lived dark-sector particles that decay to a stable DM particle and a light boson, which decays to quarks. The analysis is based on data collected in the years 2016$-$2018 with the CMS detector at the LHC in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$. Signal candidates feature large-radius jets, which are identified using a jet substructure tagger based on a graph neural network. The large-radius jet aims to reconstruct the decay of light DM mediators into four quarks, which are produced in association with two stable DM particles. The standard model background contributions are estimated from data using dedicated control regions. The missing transverse momentum spectrum is probed for a potential signal over the expected background. No significant excess over the standard model expectation is observed. Upper limits at 95% confidence level are set on the signal strength as functions of either the mediator mass or the relevant coupling. This is the first search for a pair of nonprompt DM candidates in the Lorentz-boosted topology, characterized by a large-radius jet and large missing transverse momentum.

</details>


### [111] [Antineutron reconstruction in electromagnetic calorimeters with mixed-representation learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11139)
*Yangu Li,Hongtian Yu,Yuyang Huang,Zhi Cao,Yunxuan Song,Yunfan Liu,Yajun Mao,YangHeng Zheng,Xiao-Rui Lyu,Qixiang Ye*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理启发的混合表征网络MrCAL，用于在电磁量能器（ECAL）中重建GeV尺度反中子，首次仅凭ECAL读出实现动量大小直接测量，动量方向精度提升达96%，动量分辨率达17%@1 GeV/c。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GeV尺度加速器实验中，长寿命中性强子（如反中子）在传统ECAL中的重建长期受限，因其强子-核相互作用超出ECAL原生响应范围；且反中子在ECAL中呈现两种典型能量沉积模式，亟需物理驱动的表征方法。

Method: 提出Mixed-representation Calorimetric Network（MrCAL），融合视觉表征（对应空间能量沉积图像）与序列化表征（对应纵向沉积剖面），嵌入统一目标检测架构，联合回归粒子身份、动量方向（θ, φ）及动量大小p。训练数据基于真实对撞数据集，未依赖模拟。

Result: 反中子动量方向重建精度提升最高达96%；首次仅用ECAL读出实现动量大小直接回归，1 GeV/c处分辨率σ_p/p ≈ 17%；模型在多类物理过程与背景环境下通过泛化性验证。

Conclusion: MrCAL为现有大型实验设施的遗留ECAL系统赋予新型中性强子末态探测能力，拓展其在超出标准模型新物理、奇异强子态等领域的科学覆盖范围。

Abstract: A long-standing bottleneck in GeV-scale accelerator experiments lies in reconstructing long-lived neutral hadrons in conventional electromagnetic calorimeters (ECALs), where hadron--nucleus interactions fall outside the detector's native response regime. In this paper, we develop a physics-inspired representation approach for antineutron reconstruction using a large corpus of real collision data. Motivated by two distinct energy deposition patterns from the penetrating high energy antineutrons in ECALs, we propose a Mixed-representation Calorimetric Network (MrCAL) that integrates complementary visual and sequential representation branches within a unified object-detection architecture. This architecture jointly predicts particle identity, momentum direction, and momentum magnitude. Our approach improves the precision of antineutron momentum-direction reconstruction by up to 96% and, for the first time, enables direct measurement of momentum magnitude solely from ECAL readouts, achieving a momentum resolution of approximately 17% at 1 GeV/c. The model maintains robust performance through comprehensive generalization tests spanning a wide variety of physics processes and background environments. This work unlocks a novel measurement capability for legacy ECAL systems at large experimental facilities, broadening their scientific scope via innovative final-state neutral-hadron detection.

</details>

Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文首次观测到η_c→p̄pη衰变，显著性超过10σ；通过BESIII实验中2712.4×10⁶个ψ(3686)事例，测量了该衰变的分支比，并系统考虑了η_c共振过程与非共振过程ψ(3686)→γp̄pη之间的干涉效应，给出建设性与破坏性干涉两种情形下的联合分支比及纯η_c衰变分支比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验粲偶素η_c强衰变机制，特别是重子对产生过程中的QCD手征动力学与非微扰效应；厘清共振与非共振振幅干涉对分支比提取的影响，以提升强子衰变理论模型（如光锥求和规则、格点QCD）的约束精度。

Method: 基于BESIII探测器采集的2712.4×10⁶个ψ(3686)事例，利用单光子辐射跃迁ψ(3686)→γη_c产生η_c，再重建η_c→p̄pη末态；采用部分波分析与干涉拟合方法，同时拟合共振η_c信号与非共振背景ψ(3686)→γp̄pη，通过似然函数区分建设性与破坏性干涉解。

Result: 首次观测到η_c→p̄pη衰变（>10σ）；测得联合分支比B(ψ(3686)→γη_c)×B(η_c→p̄pη) = (3.2±0.1±0.9)×10⁻⁶（建设性）或(8.7±0.3±2.1)×10⁻⁶（破坏性）；由此导出B(η_c→p̄pη) = (0.90±0.04±0.21±0.13)×10⁻³ 或 (2.42±0.07±0.48±0.34)×10⁻³，第三误差源于B(ψ(3686)→γη_c)不确定性。

Conclusion: η_c→p̄pη衰变被明确确立，其分支比对QCD因子化假设与重子对产生机制构成关键实验约束；干涉效应不可忽略，必须在提取纯共振衰变参数时显式建模，为后续粲偶素强衰变研究树立了精密干涉分析范式。

Abstract: The decay $η_c\to p\bar{p}η$ is observed for the first time with a significance of exceeding $10σ$. It is found by analyzing $(2712.4 \pm 14.3)\times10^{6}$ $ψ(3686)$ events accumulated at the BESIII detector. The measured branching fraction of $η_c\to p\bar{p}η$ via $ψ(3686) \to γp \bar{p} η$ is significantly influenced by the interference between the resonant $η_c$ decay and the non-resonant process $ψ(3686) \to γp \bar{p} η$ and is measured in both constructive- and destructive-interference scenarios. The joint branching fraction of $ψ(3686)\to γη_c$, $η_c\to p\bar{p}η$ is measured to be $(3.2 \pm 0.1 \pm 0.9)\times10^{-6}$ or $(8.7 \pm 0.3 \pm 2.1)\times10^{-6}$ for constructive- or destructive-interference solutions, respectively, where the first uncertainties are statistical and the second systematic. The branching fraction of $η_c\to p\bar{p}η$ is determined to be $\mathcal{B}(η_c\to p\bar{p}η)=(0.90 \pm 0.04 \pm 0.21 \pm 0.13)\times10^{-3}$ or $(2.42 \pm 0.07 \pm 0.48 \pm 0.34)\times10^{-3}$ for the two solutions, respectively, where the third uncertainties are due to the uncertainty in the branching fraction of $ψ(3686)\to γη_c$.

</details>


### [113] [Study of the proccess $e^+e^-\to π^+π^-π^0π^0η$ in the c.m. energy range 1.6--2.0 GeV with the CMD-3 detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11556)
*R. R. Akhmetshin,A. N. Amirkhanov,A. V. Anisenkova,V. M. Aulchenkoa,N. S. Bashtovoy,E. V. Bedarev,D. E. Berkaev,A. E. Bondar,A. V. Bragin,V. S. Denisov,D. A. Epifanov,L. B. Epshteyn,A. L. Erofeev,G. V. Fedotovich,A. O. Gorkovenko,A. A. Grebenuk,S. S. Gribanov,D. N. Grigoriev,F. V. Ignatov,V. L. Ivanov,A. S. Kasaev,S. V. Karpov,V. F. Kazanin,I. A. Koop,A. A. Korobov,A. N. Kozyrev,P. P. Krokovny,A. S. Kuzmin,I. B. Logashenko,P. A. Lukin,K. Yu. Mikhailov,I. V. Obraztsov,Yu. N. Pestov,E. A. Perevedentsev,V. G. Petrochenko,N. A. Petrov,A. S. Popov,Yu. A. Rogovsky,A. A. Ruban,A. E. Ryzhenenkov,A. V. Semenov,A. I. Senchenko,V. E. Shebalin,D. N. Shemyakin,B. A. Shwartz,D. B. Shwartz,E. P. Solodov,M. V. Timoshenko,V. M. Titov,A. A. Talyshev,S. S. Tolmachev,A. I. Vorobiov,I. M. Zemlyansky,D. S. Zhadan,Yu. V. Yudin*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用CMD-3探测器在VEPP-2000对撞机上采集的372 pb⁻¹积分亮度数据，测量了e⁺e⁻ → π⁺π⁻π⁰π⁰η过程的截面，发现其主导产生机制为ω(782)π⁰η末态，并观测到ρ(770)±在π±π⁰不变质量谱中的信号及可能的a₀(980)→π⁰η贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测量多介子末态（尤其是含η介子的四π末态）的e⁺e⁻湮灭截面，有助于理解强子谱、共振态耦合与非微扰QCD动力学，特别是矢量介子与标量介子的激发与衰变机制。

Method: 基于CMD-3实验数据，采用不变质量谱拟合与背景建模，在1.6–2.0 GeV质心能量区间内提取信号事例数（6300±145），并评估约10%的系统误差；通过π⁰η、π±π⁰等子通道的不变质量分布分析共振结构。

Result: 首次在该能量区间给出e⁺e⁻ → π⁺π⁻π⁰π⁰η的精确截面测量；确认ω(782)π⁰η为支配性中间态；观测到ρ(770)± → π±π⁰显著信号；发现a₀(980) → π⁰η可能存在小贡献。

Conclusion: 该过程由矢量主导（ωπ⁰η）且含明显ρ共振成分，暗示存在非共振连续项与标量介子参与的复杂强子化机制，为理解η-π⁰关联及a₀/σ类标量态性质提供关键实验约束。

Abstract: The cross section of the process $e^+e^- \to π^+π^-π^0π^0η$ has been measured using a data sample with the integrated luminosity of 372 pb$^{-1}$ collected with the CMD-3 detector at the VEPP-2000 $e^+e^-$ collider. 6300$\pm$145 signal events have been selected in the center-of-mass energy range 1.6--2.0 GeV. The total systematic uncertainty of the cross section is about 10\%. The production dynamics is dominated by the $ω(782)π^0η$ final state with a possible small contribution of the $a_0(980)$ resonance in the $π^0η$ combination. We also observe a presence of the $ρ(770)^{\pm}$ resonance signal in the $π^{\pm}π^0$ invariant mass distribution.

</details>


### [114] [Machine Learning-Based Reconstruction for Resistive Silicon Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11585)
*Alexander Aoki,Gaetano Barone,Leena Diehl,Gabriele Giacomini,Vagelis Gkougkousis,Hanshal Goyal,Rohan Kher,Daniel Li,Anna Macchiolo,Yevhenii Padnuik,Daria Senina,Samantha Sunnarborg,Jessica Tang,Alessandro Tricoli,Lixing Wang,Don C. Wong*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文研究基于机器学习的电阻型硅传感器（如AC-LGAD）信号重建与压缩方法，利用全波形信息提升空间分辨率至~10 μm，并提出LSTM与坐标感知Transformer架构以支持任意像素几何与FPGA部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AC-LGAD中电荷共享导致信号弱、信噪比低、矩阵反演计算复杂且噪声敏感，传统读出方法难以兼顾高精度定位与带宽限制。

Method: 采用全波形多通道时序数据输入，构建LSTM-based RNN模型（已验证HLS FPGA实现），并发展波形栅格化、窗口选择等带宽压缩策略；进一步设计拓扑无关、嵌入pad坐标的Transformer架构。

Result: 实现了~10 μm位置分辨率（针对500 μm pitch传感器），支持任意pad数目与几何排布，抑制边缘畸变，且模型具备FPGA部署可行性。

Conclusion: 机器学习（特别是时序建模与几何感知Transformer）可有效克服AC-LGAD中电荷共享带来的重建瓶颈，在保持高空间分辨率的同时显著降低前端带宽需求，为未来四维探测器设计提供新范式。

Abstract: Low-Gain Avalanche Diodes (LGADs) and AC-coupled Low-Gain Avalanche Diodes (AC-LGADs) are promising technologies for precision timing and four-dimensional tracking. In AC-LGADs, the AC pad is coupled to the resistive n$^{+}$ layer through a dielectric layer, while the gain layer remains unsegmented. This structure provides a 100\% fill factor and enables good spatial resolution with a relaxed readout pitch. The same signal-sharing mechanism that makes interpolation possible complicates the readout: charge spreads across multiple pads, the useful information can approach the electronic-noise threshold, and matrix-inversion approaches can become computationally challenging and sensitive to off-diagonal noise. In this work, we study machine-learning-based reconstruction and compression for resistive silicon sensors. We use full-waveform information from correlated pads to regularise the reconstruction and extract spatial information beyond what is available from binary readouts or reduced-amplitude summaries. We first introduce recurrent neural network models based on LSTM layers, which provide a proof-of-concept implementation for full-waveform reconstruction and have been tested for FPGA deployment using \hls. We also study routes towards bandwidth reduction with waveform rasterisation and window-selection methods, and extend the approach beyond the first model to topology-agnostic transformer-based architectures that use pad coordinates as part of the input. These models are designed to support arbitrary pad counts and geometries, mitigate edge distortions, preserve approximately $10~μ\mathrm{m}$ position resolution for $500~μ\mathrm{m}\times500~μ\mathrm{m}$ pitched sensors, and guide future resistive-silicon sensor designs

</details>


### [115] [Analytical Markov Chain for Spatiotemporal Flux Evolution of the Inner Filter Effect in Fluorescent Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.11815)
*Xuhui Yang,Guofu Cao*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时空解耦与拉普拉斯变换的解析马尔可夫输运模型，用于高效模拟多组分荧光介质中由内滤效应（IFE）引起的波长演化失真，将计算复杂度从指数级降至线性级，并实现亚秒级瞬态衰减谱与稳态光谱失真的精确预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确表征多组分荧光介质的发射与衰减时间谱对识别本征材料性质和优化探测器至关重要，但二级内滤效应（IFE）引起的波长演化会严重扭曲可观测光谱；传统蒙特卡洛（MC）方法虽能模拟该失真，却因统计累积耗时过长，难以支撑高维参数空间的快速优化。

Method: 构建基于时空解耦的解析马尔可夫输运模型；通过拉普拉斯变换将连续域上的多重卷积积分转化为离散马尔可夫转移矩阵；利用Gamma波包的连续叠加表征瞬态衰减时间谱；解析求解稳态IFE驱动的波长谱畸变。

Result: 计算复杂度由O(N_λ^n)降至O(N_λ + n)；亚秒级完成瞬态衰减谱与稳态光谱失真的全解析预测；在正交与前向面光谱仪构型下，谱形与MC模拟高度一致（lineshape match）。

Conclusion: 该模型可作为快速前向模拟引擎，显著加速参数空间扫描、提供早期探测器设计依据，并为事件顶点重建算法提供物理约束的输入。

Abstract: Characterizing emission and decay time spectra in multi-component fluorescent media is essential for identifying intrinsic material properties and optimizing detectors. However, wavelength evolution from the secondary inner filter effect (IFE) distorts these observable spectra. While Monte Carlo (MC) ray-tracing can simulate this distortion, accumulating adequate tracking statistics requires long computation times, which hinders parameter optimization within high-dimensional spaces. This paper presents an analytical Markovian transport model based on spatiotemporal decoupling. A Laplace transform converts the multi-nested convolution integrals over continuous domains into a discrete Markov transition matrix, reducing the computational complexity from an exponential scale with respect to wavelength bins $N_λ$ and cascade order $n$, $\mathcal{O}(N_λ^n)$, to a linear scale, $\mathcal{O}(N_λ + n)$. The resulting algebraic solutions evaluate transient decay time spectra as a continuum superposition of Gamma wave packets and predict steady-state wavelength spectrum distortions driven by the IFE within a sub-second timescale. Validations across orthogonal and front-face spectrometer configurations show that the calculated spectra match MC simulations in lineshape. This model can serve as a fast forward engine to accelerate parameter space screening, provide early-stage detector design references, and act as a physics-constrained input for event vertex reconstruction algorithms.

</details>
